   156→const AUTH_PARAM = PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';
   157→
   158→// Column display names
   159→const COLUMN_LABELS = {
   160→  id: 'ID', device_id: 'Device ID', device_name: 'Device Name',
   161→  tube: 'Tube', transport: 'Transport', country: 'Country',
   162→  value: 'Value', unit: 'Unit', lat: 'Lat', lon: 'Lon',
   163→  measured_at: 'Measured At', fetched_at: 'Fetched At', extra: 'Extra'
   164→};
   165→

   167→const EXPANDABLE = new Set(['extra', 'device_name']);
   168→
   169→let currentSort = 'fetched_at';
   170→let currentOrder = 'desc';
   171→let currentSearch = '';
   172→let currentOffset = 0;
   173→let currentLimit = 50;
   174→let totalRows = 0;

   195→}

   203→function clearSearch() {
   204→  document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
   205→  currentSearch = '';
   206→  currentOffset = 0;

   208→}
   209→
   210→function exportCSV() {
   211→  let url = '/api/admin/realtime/export';
   212→  url += PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';
   213→  if (currentSearch) url += (PASSWORD ? '&' : '?') + 'search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);

   215→}
   216→
   217→function sortBy(col) {
   218→  if (resizing) return;
   219→  if (currentSort === col) {
   220→    currentOrder = currentOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
   221→  } else {
   222→    currentSort = col;
   223→    currentOrder = 'desc';
   224→  }
   225→  currentOffset = 0;
   226→  fetchData();
   227→}
   228→
   229→function formatUnixTimestamp(val) {
   230→  if (!val) return '';
   231→  try {
   232→    const num = Number(val);
   233→    if (isNaN(num) || num <= 0) return String(val);
   234→    const d = new Date(num * 1000);
   235→    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(val);
   236→    return d.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, ' UTC');
   237→  } catch { return String(val); }
   238→}
   239→
   240→async function fetchData() {
   241→  const loading = document.getElementById('loading');
   242→  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');
   243→  const noData = document.getElementById('noData');
   244→
   245→  loading.style.display = 'block';
   246→  table.style.display = 'none';
   247→  noData.style.display = 'none';
   248→
   249→  let url = `/api/admin/realtime/data?limit=${currentLimit}&offset=${currentOffset}&sort=${currentSort}&order=${currentOrder}`;
   250→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   251→  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
   252→
   253→  try {
   254→    const resp = await fetch(url);
   255→    if (!resp.ok) {
   256→      if (resp.status === 401) {
   257→        loading.textContent = 'Unauthorized - please login as admin or provide password';
   258→        return;
   259→      }
   260→      try {
   261→        const errJson = await resp.json();
   262→        if (errJson.error) {
   263→          loading.textContent = errJson.error;
   264→          return;
   265→        }
   266→      } catch {}
   267→      throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   268→    }
   269→
   270→    const json = await resp.json();
   271→    totalRows = json.total;
   272→    const columns = json.columns || [];
   273→    const data = json.data || [];
   274→
   275→    loading.style.display = 'none';
   276→
   277→    if (data.length === 0) {
   278→      noData.style.display = 'block';
   279→      document.getElementById('summary').textContent = 'No results';
   280→      document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';
   281→      return;
   282→    }
   283→
   284→    currentData = data;
   285→    currentColumns = columns;
   286→
   287→    // Build header
   288→    const thead = document.getElementById('tableHead');
   289→    let headerHTML = '<tr><th class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" onchange="toggleAll(this)"></th>';
   290→    columns.forEach(col => {
   291→      const label = COLUMN_LABELS[col] || col;
   292→      const sortClass = currentSort === col ? (currentOrder === 'asc' ? 'sortable asc' : 'sortable desc') : 'sortable';
   293→      headerHTML += `<th class="${sortClass}" onclick="sortBy('${col}')">${label}<span class="resize-handle"></span></th>`;
   294→    });
   295→    headerHTML += '</tr>';
   296→    // Filter row
   297→    headerHTML += '<tr class="filter-row"><th></th>';
   298→    columns.forEach(col => {
   299→      const label = COLUMN_LABELS[col] || col;
   300→      headerHTML += `<th><input type="text" class="filter-input" placeholder="${label}..." onkeyup="filterTable()"></th>`;
   301→    });
   302→    headerHTML += '</tr>';
   303→    thead.innerHTML = headerHTML;
   304→
   305→    // Build body
   306→    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
   307→    let bodyHTML = '';
   308→    data.forEach((row, idx) => {
   309→      const keyVal = row['id'];
   310→      bodyHTML += `<tr><td class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" class="row-cb" value="${keyVal}" onchange="updateDeleteBtn()"></td>`;
   311→      columns.forEach(col => {
   312→        let val = row[col];
   313→        if (val === null || val === undefined) val = '';
   314→        const strVal = String(val);
   315→        if (EXPANDABLE.has(col) && strVal.length > 60) {
   316→          const preview = strVal.substring(0, 60) + '...';
   317→          bodyHTML += `<td class="expandable" title="Click to expand" onclick="showModal('${COLUMN_LABELS[col] || col}', ${JSON.stringify(strVal).replace(/'/g, "&#39;")})">${escapeHtml(preview)}</td>`;
   318→        } else if (col === 'measured_at' || col === 'fetched_at') {
   319→          bodyHTML += `<td>${formatUnixTimestamp(val)}</td>`;
   320→        } else if (col === 'value') {
   321→          bodyHTML += `<td>${typeof val === 'number' ? val.toFixed(4) : escapeHtml(strVal)}</td>`;
   322→        } else if (col === 'lat' || col === 'lon') {
   323→          bodyHTML += `<td>${typeof val === 'number' ? val.toFixed(6) : escapeHtml(strVal)}</td>`;
   324→        } else {
   325→          bodyHTML += `<td>${escapeHtml(strVal)}</td>`;
   326→        }
   327→      });
   328→      bodyHTML += '</tr>';
   329→    });
   330→    tbody.innerHTML = bodyHTML;
   331→    table.style.display = 'table';
   332→    initColumnResize();
   333→    updateDeleteBtn();
   334→
   335→    // Summary
   336→    const page = Math.floor(currentOffset / currentLimit) + 1;
   337→    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / currentLimit);
   338→    document.getElementById('summary').textContent = `${totalRows} total rows | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;
   339→
   340→    buildPagination(totalPages, page);
   341→
   342→  } catch (err) {
   343→    loading.textContent = 'Error loading data: ' + err.message;
   344→  }
   345→}
   346→
   347→function buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {
   348→  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');
   349→  let html = '';
   350→
   351→  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(1)">First</button>`;
   352→  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage - 1})">Prev</button>`;
   353→
   354→  let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 3);
   355→  let end = Math.min(totalPages, start + 6);
   356→  start = Math.max(1, end - 6);
   357→
   358→  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
   359→    html += `<button class="${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="goToPage(${i})">${i}</button>`;
   360→  }
   361→
   362→  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage + 1})">Next</button>`;
   363→  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${totalPages})">Last</button>`;
   364→
   365→  html += ` <select onchange="changeLimit(this.value)">`;
   366→  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {
   367→    html += `<option value="${n}" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;
   368→  });
   369→  html += `</select>`;
   370→
   371→  container.innerHTML = html;
   372→}
   373→
   374→function goToPage(page) {
   375→  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;
   376→  fetchData();
   377→}
   378→
   379→function changeLimit(val) {
   380→  currentLimit = parseInt(val);
   381→  currentOffset = 0;
   382→  fetchData();
   383→}
   384→
   385→function showModal(title, content) {
   386→  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;
   387→  document.getElementById('modalContent').textContent = content;
   388→  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');
   389→}
   390→
   391→function closeModal() {
   392→  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('visible');
   393→}
   394→
   395→function escapeHtml(str) {
   396→  const div = document.createElement('div');
   397→  div.textContent = str;
   398→  return div.innerHTML;
   399→}
   400→
   401→function toggleAll(master) {
   402→  document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = master.checked; });
   403→  updateDeleteBtn();
   404→}
   405→
   406→function filterTable() {
   407→  const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
   408→  const filters = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-input');
   409→  const rows = tbody.querySelectorAll('tr');
   410→  rows.forEach(row => {
   411→    let show = true;
   412→    filters.forEach((filter, index) => {
   413→      const val = filter.value.toLowerCase();
   414→      if (val) {
   415→        const cell = row.cells[index + 1]; // +1 for checkbox column
   416→        if (cell) {
   417→          if (!cell.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(val)) show = false;
   418→        }
   419→      }
   420→    });
   421→    row.style.display = show ? '' : 'none';
   422→  });
   423→}
   424→
   425→function updateDeleteBtn() {
   426→  const checked = document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb:checked').length;
   427→  const btn = document.getElementById('deleteSelectedBtn');
   428→  btn.disabled = checked === 0;
   429→  btn.textContent = checked > 0 ? `Delete Selected (${checked})` : 'Delete Selected';
   430→}
   431→
   432→async function deleteSelected() {
   433→  const checked = document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb:checked');
   434→  if (checked.length === 0) return;
   435→  if (!confirm(`Delete ${checked.length} selected row(s)?`)) return;
   436→
   437→  const ids = Array.from(checked).map(cb => cb.value).join(',');
   438→  let url = `/api/admin/realtime/delete?ids=${encodeURIComponent(ids)}`;
   439→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   440→
   441→  try {
   442→    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
   443→    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   444→    const json = await resp.json();
   445→    alert(`Deleted ${json.deleted} row(s)`);
   446→    fetchData();
   447→  } catch (err) {
   448→    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);
   449→  }
   450→}
   451→
   452→async function deleteAll() {
   453→  const scope = currentSearch ? 'all filtered rows' : 'ALL rows';
   454→  if (!confirm(`Delete ${scope} from realtime_measurements? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
   455→
   456→  let url = `/api/admin/realtime/delete?all=true`;
   457→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   458→  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
   459→
   460→  try {
   461→    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
   462→    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   463→    const json = await resp.json();
   464→    alert(`Deleted ${json.deleted} row(s)`);
   465→    fetchData();
   466→  } catch (err) {
   467→    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);
   468→  }
   469→}
   470→
   471→let columnWidths = {};
   472→let resizing = false;
   473→
   474→function initColumnResize() {
   475→  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');
   476→  if (!table) return;
   477→  const headerRow = table.querySelector('thead tr:first-child');
   478→  if (!headerRow) return;
   479→  const ths = headerRow.querySelectorAll('th');
   480→
   481→  ths.forEach((th, i) => {
   482→    if (columnWidths[i]) {
   483→      th.style.width = columnWidths[i];
   484→    } else {
   485→      th.style.width = th.offsetWidth + 'px';
   486→    }
   487→  });
   488→
   489→  document.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle').forEach(handle => {
   490→    handle.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
   491→      e.preventDefault();
   492→      e.stopPropagation();
   493→      resizing = true;
   494→      const th = this.parentElement;
   495→      const colIndex = Array.from(th.parentElement.children).indexOf(th);
   496→      const startX = e.pageX;
   497→      const startWidth = th.offsetWidth;
   498→      this.classList.add('active');
   499→

<option value=\"\">All languages</option>\n  </select>\n  <input type=\"text\" id=\"searchInput\" placeholder=\"Search key or value...\" onkeydown=\"if(event.key==='Enter')doSearch()\">\n  <button onclick=\"doSearch()\">Search</button>\n  <button onclick=\"clearSearch()\">Clear</button>\n  <button class=\"add-btn\" onclick=\"showAddModal()\">+ Add Translation</button>\n  <button class=\"reload-btn\" onclick=\"reloadTranslations()\">Reload into Memory</button>\n  <span class=\"summary\" id=\"summary\"></span>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"table-wrap\">\n  <div class=\"loading\" id=\"loading\">Loading...</div>\n  <table id=\"dataTable\" style=\"display:none\">\n    <thead>\n      <tr>\n        <th class=\"col-id sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('id')\">ID</th>\n        <th class=\"col-lang sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('language_code')\">Lang</th>\n        <th class=\"col-key sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('key')\">Key</th>\n        <th class=\"col-value\">Value</th>\n        <th class=\"col-date sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('updated_at')\">Updated</th>\n        <th class=\"col-actions\">Actions</th>\n      </tr>\n    </thead>\n    <tbody id=\"tableBody\"></tbody>\n  </table>\n  <div class=\"no-data\" id=\"noData\" style=\"display:none\">No translations found</div>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"pagination\" id=\"pagination\"></div>\n\n<!-- Edit/Add Modal -->\n<div class=\"modal-overlay\" id=\"modalOverlay\" onclick=\"closeModal()\">\n  <div class=\"modal\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation()\">\n    <h3 id=\"modalTitle\">Edit Translation</h3>\n    <input type=\"hidden\" id=\"editId\">\n    <label for=\"editLang\">Language Code</label>\n    <input type=\"text\" id=\"editLang\" placeholder=\"e.g. en, cs, ja\" maxlength=\"10\">\n    <label for=\"editKey\">Key</label>\n    <input type=\"text\" id=\"editKey\" placeholder=\"e.g. legend_safe\">\n    <label for=\"editValue\">Value</label>\n    <textarea id=\"editValue\" placeholder=\"Translation text...\"></textarea>\n    <div class=\"btn-row\">\n      <button class=\"btn-cancel\" onclick=\"closeModal()\">Cancel</button>\n      <button class=\"btn-save\" onclick=\"saveTranslation()\">Save</button>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</div>\n\n<script>\nconst PASSWORD = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('password') || '';\nconst AUTH_PARAM = PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';\n\nlet currentSort = 'key';\nlet currentOrder = 'asc';\nlet currentSearch = '';\nlet currentLang = '';\nlet currentOffset = 0;\nlet currentLimit = 50;\nlet totalRows = 0;\nlet availableLanguages = [];\n\nfunction buildAdminTabs() {\n  const tabs = [\n    { label: 'Users', href: '/admin/users' },\n    { label: 'Uploads', href: '/admin/uploads' },\n    { label: 'MCP Analytics', href: '/admin/mcp' },\n    { label: 'Realtime', href: '/admin/realtime' },\n    { label: 'Translations', href: '/admin/translations', active: true }\n  ];\n  const container = document.getElementById('adminTabs');\n  tabs.forEach(t => {\n    const href = t.href + (PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '');\n    container.innerHTML += `<a href=\"${href}\" class=\"${t.active ? 'active' : ''}\">${t.label}</a>`;\n  });\n}\n\nfunction doSearch() {\n  currentSearch = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.trim();\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction clearSearch() {\n  document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';\n  currentSearch = '';\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction filterByLang() {\n  currentLang = document.getElementById('langFilter').value;\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction sortBy(col) {\n  if (currentSort === col) {\n    currentOrder = currentOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';\n  } else {\n    currentSort = col;\n    currentOrder = col === 'key' || col === 'language_code' ? 'asc' : 'desc';\n  }\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n  updateSortIndicators();\n}\n\nfunction updateSortIndicators() {\n  document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {\n    th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');\n  });\n  // Find the matching th\n  document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {\n    const col = th.getAttribute('onclick')?.match(/sortBy\\('(\\w+)'\\)/)?.[1];\n    if (col === currentSort) {\n      th.classList.add(currentOrder);\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(str) {\n  const div = document.createElement('div');\n  div.textContent = str;\n  return div.innerHTML;\n}\n\nfunction formatDate(val) {\n  if (!val) return '';\n  try {\n    const d = new Date(val);\n    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(val);\n    return d.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').replace(/\\.\\d{3}Z$/, '');\n  } catch { return String(val); }\n}\n\nasync function fetchData() {\n  const loading = document.getElementById('loading');\n  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');\n  const noData = document.getElementById('noData');\n\n  loading.style.display = 'block';\n  table.style.display = 'none';\n  noData.style.display = 'none';\n\n  let url = `/api/admin/translations?limit=${currentLimit}&offset=${currentOffset}&sort=${currentSort}&order=${currentOrder}`;\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);\n  if (currentLang) url += '&lang=' + encodeURIComponent(currentLang);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url);\n    if (!resp.ok) {\n      if (resp.status === 401) {\n        loading.textContent = 'Unauthorized - please login as admin or provide password';\n        return;\n      }\n      throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    }\n\n    const json = await resp.json();\n    totalRows = json.total;\n    const data = json.data || [];\n\n    // Populate language dropdown if we have languages\n    if (json.languages && json.languages.length > 0 && availableLanguages.length === 0) {\n      availableLanguages = json.languages;\n      const select = document.getElementById('langFilter');\n      json.languages.forEach(lang => {\n        const opt = document.createElement('option');\n        opt.value = lang;\n        opt.textContent = lang;\n        if (lang === currentLang) opt.selected = true;\n        select.appendChild(opt);\n      });\n    }\n\n    loading.style.display = 'none';\n\n    if (data.length === 0) {\n      noData.style.display = 'block';\n      document.getElementById('summary').textContent = 'No results';\n      document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Build body\n    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');\n    let html = '';\n    data.forEach(row => {\n      const valuePreview = row.value.length > 120 ? row.value.substring(0, 120) + '...' : row.value;\n      html += `<tr>\n        <td class=\"col-id\">${row.id}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-lang\"><span class=\"lang-badge\">${escapeHtml(row.language_code)}</span></td>\n        <td class=\"col-key\">${escapeHtml(row.key)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-value\" title=\"Click to edit\" onclick=\"showEditModal(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}', ${JSON.stringify(row.value)})\">${escapeHtml(valuePreview)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-date\">${formatDate(row.updated_at)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-actions\">\n          <button class=\"btn-edit\" onclick=\"showEditModal(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}', ${JSON.stringify(row.value)})\">Edit</button>\n          <button class=\"btn-delete\" onclick=\"deleteTranslation(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}')\">Del</button>\n        </td>\n      </tr>`;\n    });\n    tbody.innerHTML = html;\n    table.style.display = 'table';\n    updateSortIndicators();\n\n    const page = Math.floor(currentOffset / currentLimit) + 1;\n    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / currentLimit);\n    document.getElementById('summary').textContent = `${totalRows} translations | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;\n    buildPagination(totalPages, page);\n\n  } catch (err) {\n    loading.textContent = 'Error loading data: ' + err.message;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction escapeAttr(str) {\n  return str.replace(/'/g, \"\\\\'\").replace(/\"/g, '&quot;');\n}\n\nfunction buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {\n  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');\n  let html = '';\n  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(1)\">First</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${currentPage - 1})\">Prev</button>`;\n  let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 3);\n  let end = Math.min(totalPages, start + 6);\n  start = Math.max(1, end - 6);\n  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {\n    html += `<button class=\"${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}\" onclick=\"goToPage(${i})\">${i}</button>`;\n  }\n  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${currentPage + 1})\">Next</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${totalPages})\">Last</button>`;\n  html += ` <select onchange=\"changeLimit(this.value)\">`;\n  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {\n    html += `<option value=\"${n}\" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;\n  });\n  html += `</select>`;\n  container.innerHTML = html;\n}\n\nfunction goToPage(page) {\n  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction changeLimit(val) {\n  currentLimit = parseInt(val);\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction showEditModal(id, lang, key, value) {\n  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Edit Translation';\n  document.getElementById('editId').value = id;\n  document.getElementById('editLang').value = lang;\n  document.getElementById('editLang').readOnly = true;\n  document.getElementById('editKey').value = key;\n  document.getElementById('editKey').readOnly = true;\n  document.getElementById('editValue').value = value;\n  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');\n  document.getElementById('editValue').focus();\n}\n\nfunction showAddModal() {\n  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Add Translation';\n  document.getElementById('editId').value = '';\n  document.getElementById('editLang').value = currentLang || '';\n  document.getElementById('editLang').readOnly = false;\n  document.getElementById('editKey').value = '';\n  document.getElementById('editKey').readOnly = false;\n  document.getElementById('editValue').value = '';\n  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');\n  document.getElementById('editLang').focus();\n}\n\nfunction closeModal() {\n  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('visible');\n}\n\nasync function saveTranslation() {\n  const id = document.getElementById('editId').value;\n  const lang = document.getElementById('editLang').value.trim();\n  const key = document.getElementById('editKey').value.trim();\n  const value = document.getElementById('editValue').value;\n\n  if (!lang || !key) {\n    alert('Language code and key are required');\n    return;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    let url, method, body;\n    if (id) {\n      // Update existing\n      url = `/api/admin/translations/${id}`;\n      if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n      method = 'PUT';\n      body = JSON.stringify({ value });\n    } else {\n      // Create new\n      url = '/api/admin/translations';\n      if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n      method = 'POST';\n      body = JSON.stringify({ language_code: lang, key, value });\n    }\n\n    const resp = await fetch(url, {\n      method,\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body\n    });\n\n    if (!resp.ok) {\n      const text = await resp.text();\n      throw new Error(text || 'HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    }\n\n    closeModal();\n    fetchData();\n  } catch (err) {\n    alert('Save failed: ' + err.message);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function deleteTranslation(id, lang, key) {\n  if (!confirm(`Delete translation ${lang}.${key}?`)) return;\n\n  let url = `/api/admin/translations/${id}`;\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });\n    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    fetchData();\n  } catch (err) {\n    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function reloadTranslations() {\n  let url = '/api/admin/translations/reload';\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST' });\n    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    const json = await resp.json();\n    alert(json.message || 'Translations reloaded!');\n  } catch (err) {\n    alert('Reload failed: ' + err.message);\n  }\n}\n\n// Keyboard shortcut: Ctrl+S to save in modal\ndocument.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {\n  if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 's') {\n    if (document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.contains('visible')) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      saveTranslation();\n    }\n  }\n  if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n    closeModal();\n  }\n});\n\nbuildAdminTabs();\nfetchData();\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

> AGENT

Done! PR #28 has been merged: https://github.com/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pull/28

Here's what was completed:

- **Database storage**: Translations table in PostgreSQL (and SQLite) with auto-seeding from the existing JSON file (3,972 rows)
- **Admin UI**: Full management page at `/admin/translations` with search, language filter, pagination, inline edit, create, delete, and live reload
- **Translations tab**: Added to all admin page navigation bars (Users, Uploads, Tracks, MCP Analytics, Realtime)
- **Backward compatible**: Falls back to embedded JSON if DB is unavailable; auto-seeds on first run

The GitHub Actions deployment will automatically build and deploy to production. After deployment, you can manage translations directly from the admin UI without needing to rebuild the binary.

> DEVELOPER

The translation are now also done for the widget and teh AI part of the map?

> AGENT

No, not yet. The work so far has been:

1. **Fixed Czech + all language translations** for existing map UI strings (duration, live_chart, live_marker, etc.)
2. **Moved translations to PostgreSQL** with the admin UI

The translations for these parts are still pending:

- **AI assistant widget** (chat interface, prompts, placeholders)
- **Auth modals** (login, register, forgot password)
- **Search/spectrum modals**
- **Profile page**
- **Admin pages**
- **Web-chat** (`cmd/web-chat/`)
- **MCP server tool descriptions** (`cmd/mcp-server/`)
- **Legend full text** for all non-English languages

These were planned as follow-up work after the DB infrastructure was in place. Now that the admin UI exists, translations can be added either through the admin page or via SQL inserts.

Would you like me to start adding translations for these parts? The AI widget and auth modals would be the most user-facing ones to tackle first.

 10262→      function getMapContext() {
 10263→        if (typeof map === 'undefined') return null;
 10264→        try {
 10265→          const center = map.getCenter();
 10266→          const bounds = map.getBounds();
 10267→          return {
 10268→            center: { lat: center.lat, lon: center.lng },
 10269→            zoom: map.getZoom(),
 10270→            bounds: {
 10271→              minLat: bounds.getSouth(),
 10272→              minLon: bounds.getWest(),
 10273→              maxLat: bounds.getNorth(),
 10274→              maxLon: bounds.getEast()
 10275→            }
 10276→          };
 10277→        } catch (e) {
 10278→          console.error("Context error:", e);
 10279→          return null;
 10280→        }
 10281→      }
 10282→
 10283→      function markdownToHTML(text) {
 10284→        if (!text) return '';
 10285→
 10286→        let html = text
 10287→          .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
 10288→          .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
 10289→          .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
 10290→          .replace(/### (.+)/g, '<h3 style="margin: 20px 0 10px 0; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700;">$1</h3>')
 10291→          .replace(/## (.+)/g, '<h2 style="margin: 24px 0 12px 0; font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700;">$1</h2>')
 10292→          .replace(/# (.+)/g, '<h1 style="margin: 28px 0 14px 0; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700;">$1</h1>')
 10293→          .replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
 10294→          .replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '<em>$1</em>')
 10295→          .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code style="background: var(--control-bg); padding: 2px 5px; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 0.9em;">$1</code>')
 10296→          .replace(/\[(.+?)\]\((.+?)\)/g, '<a href="$2" target="_blank">$1</a>');
 10297→
 10298→        // Robust Markdown Table parser
 10299→        // We match from the first pipe to the last pipe in a greedy block
 10300→        html = html.replace(/((^|\n)\|[\s\S]*?\|($|\n(?![| \t]*\|)))/g, function (match) {
 10301→          let rows = match.trim().split('\n').map(r => r.trim()).filter(r => r.length > 0);
 10302→          if (rows.length < 2) return match;
 10303→
 10304→          let out = '<div class="ai-table-container"><table class="ai-table">';
 10305→          let bodyRows = [];
 10306→
 10307→          rows.forEach((row) => {
 10308→            if (row.match(/^\|?[\s|:-]+\|?$/)) return; // separator row
 10309→
 10310→            let cols = row.split('|');
 10311→            // Remove leading/trailing empty cells if pipes were used as borders
 10312→            if (row.startsWith('|')) cols.shift();
 10313→            if (row.endsWith('|')) cols.pop();
 10314→            bodyRows.push(cols.map(c => c.trim()));
 10315→          });
 10316→
 10317→          bodyRows.forEach((cols, rowIndex) => {
 10318→            out += '<tr>';
 10319→            cols.forEach(col => {
 10320→              if (rowIndex === 0) {
 10321→                out += `<th>${col}</th>`;
 10322→              } else {
 10323→                out += `<td>${col}</td>`;
 10324→              }
 10325→            });
 10326→            out += '</tr>';
 10327→          });
 10328→
 10329→          out += '</table></div>';
 10330→          return out;
 10331→        });
 10332→
 10333→        // Handle line breaks ONLY if they are not inside a table-container
 10334→        let parts = html.split(/(<div class="ai-table-container">[\s\S]*?<\/div>)/g);
 10335→        html = parts.map(part => {
 10336→          if (part.startsWith('<div class="ai-table-container">')) return part;
 10337→          return part.replace(/\n\n/g, '<br><br>').replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
 10338→        }).join('');
 10339→
 10340→        return html;
 10341→      }

 10379→      function executeMapAction(actionData) {
 10380→        if (!actionData || typeof map === 'undefined') return;
 10381→        try {
 10382→          if (actionData.action === 'panTo' && actionData.lat !== undefined && actionData.lon !== undefined) {
 10383→            map.panTo([actionData.lat, actionData.lon]);
 10384→          } else if (actionData.action === 'zoomTo' && actionData.level !== undefined) {
 10385→            map.setZoom(actionData.level);
 10386→          } else if (actionData.action === 'highlightLocation' && actionData.lat !== undefined && actionData.lon !== undefined) {
 10387→            const marker = L.circleMarker([actionData.lat, actionData.lon], {
 10388→              radius: 20,
 10389→              color: '#FF0000',
 10390→              fillColor: '#FF0000',
 10391→              fillOpacity: 0.3
 10392→            }).addTo(map);
 10393→            setTimeout(() => {
 10394→              if (map.hasLayer(marker)) map.removeLayer(marker);
 10395→            }, 5000);
 10396→          }
 10397→        } catch (e) {
 10398→          console.error("Action execution failed:", e);
 10399→        }
 10400→      }
 10401→
 10402→      function submitMessage() {
 10403→        const text = msgInput.value.trim();
 10404→        if (!text || busy) return;
 10405→
 10406→        busy = true;
 10407→        sendBtn.disabled = true;
 10408→
 10409→        addMessageUI('user', text);
 10410→        const botBubble = addMessageUI('bot', '...thinking...');
 10411→        botBubble.classList.add('ai-thinking');
 10412→
 10413→        msgInput.value = '';
 10414→        msgInput.style.height = '44px';
 10415→
 10416→        let accumulated = '';
 10417→        function finish(success) {
 10418→          botBubble.classList.remove('ai-thinking');
 10419→          busy = false;
 10420→          sendBtn.disabled = false;
 10421→          msgInput.focus();
 10422→          if (success && accumulated) {
 10423→            chatHistory.push(
 10424→              { role: 'user', content: text },
 10425→              { role: 'assistant', content: accumulated }
 10426→            );
 10427→            if (chatHistory.length > 10) {
 10428→              chatHistory = chatHistory.slice(-10);
 10429→            }
 10430→          }
 10431→        }
 10432→
 10433→        fetch('/chat', {
 10434→          method: 'POST',
 10435→          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 10436→          body: JSON.stringify({
 10437→            message: text,
 10438→            history: chatHistory,
 10439→            map_context: getMapContext(),
 10440→            source: 'widget',
 10441→            client_timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
 10442→          }),
 10443→        }).then(response => {
 10444→          const reader = response.body.getReader();
 10445→          const decoder = new TextDecoder();
 10446→          let buffer = '';
 10447→
 10448→          function pump() {
 10449→            return reader.read().then(({ done, value }) => {
 10450→              if (done) { finish(true); return; }
 10451→
 10452→              buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
 10453→              const lines = buffer.split('\n');
 10454→              buffer = lines.pop();
 10455→
 10456→              for (const line of lines) {
 10457→                if (!line.trim()) continue;
 10458→                try {
 10459→                  const ev = JSON.parse(line);
 10460→                  if (ev.type === 'text') {
 10461→                    if (botBubble.classList.contains('ai-thinking')) {
 10462→                      botBubble.classList.remove('ai-thinking');
 10463→                      accumulated = '';
 10464→                    }
 10465→                    accumulated += ev.text;
 10466→                    botBubble.innerHTML = markdownToHTML(accumulated);
 10467→                    messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight;
 10468→                  } else if (ev.type === 'action' || ev.action) {
 10469→                    executeMapAction(ev);
 10470→                  } else if (ev.type === 'done') {
 10471→                    finish(true);
 10472→                  } else if (ev.type === 'error') {
 10473→                    botBubble.textContent = 'Error: ' + ev.error;
 10474→                    botBubble.classList.remove('ai-thinking');
 10475→                    finish(false);
 10476→                  }
 10477→                } catch (e) { }
 10478→              }
 10479→              return pump();
 10480→            });
 10481→          }
 10482→          return pump();
 10483→        }).catch(err => {
 10484→          botBubble.textContent = 'Connection error: ' + err.message;
 10485→          botBubble.classList.remove('ai-thinking');
 10486→          finish(false);
 10487→        });
 10488→      }
 10489→    })();
 10490→  </script>
 10491→
 10492→</body>
 10493→
 10494→</html>
 10495→

Merge all language data\n    all_langs = {}\n    all_langs.update(TRANSLATIONS)\n    for code, data in LANG_TRANSLATIONS.items():\n        d = dict(data)\n        d.pop(\"name\", None)\n        all_langs[code] = d\n    for code, (name, data) in REMAINING.items():\n        all_langs[code] = data\n\n    # Add email placeholder to all langs that don't have it\n    for code in all_langs:\n        if \"auth_email_placeholder\" not in all_langs[code]:\n            all_langs[code][\"auth_email_placeholder\"] = \"your@email.com\"\n\n    # Output SQL\n    print(\"-- Migration: Add UI translations (AI widget, auth, search, spectrum, profile, web-chat)\")\n    print(\"-- Generated by gen_translations.py\")\n    print(\"-- Run: psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d safecast -f migrations/add_ui_translations.sql\")\n    print()\n\n    # English first\n    for key, value in sorted(KEYS.items()):\n        print(f\"INSERT INTO translations (language_code, key, value) VALUES ('en', '{escape_sql(key)}', '{escape_sql(value)}') ON CONFLICT (language_code, key) DO UPDATE SET value = EXCLUDED.value, updated_at = NOW();\")\n\n    print()\n\n    # All other languages\n    for lang_code in sorted(all_langs.keys()):\n        data = all_langs[lang_code]\n        print(f\"-- {lang_code}\")\n        for key in sorted(KEYS.keys()):\n            if key in data:\n                value = data[key]\n            else:\n                # Fallback to English\n                value = KEYS[key]\n            print(f\"INSERT INTO translations (language_code, key, value) VALUES ('{escape_sql(lang_code)}', '{escape_sql(key)}', '{escape_sql(value)}') ON CONFLICT (language_code, key) DO UPDATE SET value = EXCLUDED.value, updated_at = NOW();\")\n        print()\n\n    # Also output JSON patch for translations.json update\n    json_patch = {\"en\": KEYS}\n    for lang_code, data in sorted(all_langs.items()):\n        json_patch[lang_code] = {}\n        for key in KEYS:\n            json_patch[lang_code][key] = data.get(key, KEYS[key])\n\n    with open(\"/tmp/translations_patch.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(json_patch, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"-- Total: {len(KEYS)} keys x {1 + len(all_langs)} languages = {len(KEYS) * (1 + len(all_langs))} rows\", file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"-- Languages with translations: en, {', '.join(sorted(all_langs.keys()))}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"-- JSON patch written to /tmp/translations_patch.json\", file=sys.stderr)\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

Для критических решений обращайтесь к официальным источникам.\",\n    \"webchat_copy\": \"Копировать\", \"webchat_copied\": \"Скопировано!\",\n    \"webchat_no_conversation\": \"Нет разговора для скачивания!\", \"webchat_thinking\": \"...думаю...\",\n}\n\n# For remaining languages (pt, it, nl, and all others), seed with English fallback\n# They can be translated later via the admin UI\nREMAINING_LANGS = [\"ar\", \"bg\", \"cs\", \"da\", \"el\", \"fa\", \"fi\", \"he\", \"hi\", \"hu\", \"id\", \"it\", \"ms\", \"nl\", \"no\", \"pl\", \"pt\", \"sv\", \"th\", \"tr\", \"uk\", \"vi\"]\n\nlines = []\nlines.append(\"-- Batch 2: fr, es, de, ru translations + English fallback for remaining languages\")\nlines.append(\"\")\n\nfor lang in sorted(T):\n    lines.append(f\"-- {lang}\")\n    for k in sorted(EN):\n        v = T[lang].get(k, EN[k])\n        lines.append(ins(lang, k, v))\n    lines.append(\"\")\n\n# Remaining languages get English values as placeholder\nfor lang in sorted(REMAINING_LANGS):\n    lines.append(f\"-- {lang} (English fallback - translate via admin UI)\")\n    for k in sorted(EN):\n        lines.append(ins(lang, k, EN[k]))\n    lines.append(\"\")\n\nwith open(\"/tmp/ui_translations_batch2.sql\", \"w\") as f:\n    f.write(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n\n# Update the patch JSON\nfor lang in T:\n    full = {}\n    for k in EN:\n        full[k] = T[lang].get(k, EN[k])\n    patch[lang] = full\n\nfor lang in REMAINING_LANGS:\n    if lang not in patch:\n        patch[lang] = dict(EN)\n\nwith open(\"/tmp/translations_patch.json\", \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(patch, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\nprint(f\"Batch 2: {sorted(T.keys())} + {len(REMAINING_LANGS)} fallback langs\")\nprint(f\"SQL: /tmp/ui_translations_batch2.sql\")\n"
}

```json
{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport json\n\n# Load existing translations\nwith open('cmd/unified-server/public_html/translations.json') as f:\n    existing = json.load(f)\n\n# Load new keys patch\nwith open('/tmp/translations_patch.json') as f:\n    patch = json.load(f)\n\n# Merge: add new keys to each language\nfor lang in patch:\n    if lang not in existing:\n        existing[lang] = {}\n    for key, value in patch[lang].items():\n        if key not in existing[lang]:\n            existing[lang][key] = value\n\n# Write back\nwith open('cmd/unified-server/public_html/translations.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(existing, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n# Also update root copy\nwith open('public_html/translations.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(existing, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n# Count new keys added\nnew_count = sum(len(patch[l]) for l in patch)\nprint(f'Merged {new_count} translations into translations.json')\nprint(f'Total languages: {len(existing)}, sample key count (en): {len(existing[\\\"en\\\"])}')\n\"",
  "description": "Merge new translations into translations.json"
}

> AGENT

Done. PR #29 merged to main: https://github.com/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pull/29

Summary of what was translated:
- **AI assistant widget** — title, subtitle, placeholder, buttons, disclaimer, greeting, thinking, error messages
- **Auth modals** — login, register, forgot password (all labels, placeholders, buttons, error/success messages)
- **User menu** — Profile, Admin, Logout
- **Search** — placeholder, clear, no results, error
- **Spectrum viewer** — title, labels, buttons, loading states
- **Profile page** — all static and dynamic strings (converted to Go template rendering)

144 new keys × 29 languages. Proper translations for en/ja/zh/ko/fr/es/de/ru; remaining 22 languages have English fallback editable via the admin UI.

> AGENT

The admin translations page is at:

**Local:** `http://localhost:8765/admin/translations?password=test123`

**Production:** `https://simplemap.safecast.org/admin/translations` (requires admin login)

From there you can:

1. **Filter by language** — dropdown with all 29 languages
2. **Search** — type a key name or value to find specific translations
3. **Edit** — click a translation row to edit the value inline
4. **Add new** — button to create a new translation key
5. **Delete** — remove a translation
6. **Reload** — after editing, click "Reload Translations" to apply changes without restarting the server

To fill in the 22 languages that currently have English fallback, you'd filter by language (e.g. "Thai"), search for a key like `ai_title`, edit the value to the Thai translation, and hit save. Then click Reload to make it live.

You can also reach it from any admin page — there's a "Translations" tab in the admin navigation bar alongside Users, Uploads, MCP Analytics, and Realtime.

  5096→	// Get limit parameter (page size)

  5100→			limit = parsedLimit
  5101→		}

  5112→	// Calculate offset

  5127→		http.Error(w, "Failed to count uploads", http.StatusInternalServerError)

  5130→
  5131→	// Calculate total pages

  5137→	// Fetch current page of uploads

  5499→
  5500→		if numWorkers == 1 {

  5502→			for i, upload := range uploads {

  5506→			// Parallel processing for large datasets
  5507→			var wg sync.WaitGroup
  5508→			batchSize := (len(uploads) + numWorkers - 1) / numWorkers
  5509→
  5510→			for w := 0; w < numWorkers; w++ {
  5511→				wg.Add(1)
  5512→				start := w * batchSize
  5513→				end := start + batchSize
  5514→				if end > len(uploads) {
  5515→					end = len(uploads)
  5516→				}
  5517→
  5518→				go func(start, end int) {
  5519→					defer wg.Done()
  5520→					for i := start; i < end; i++ {
  5521→						rows[i] = formatUploadRow(uploads[i], password)
  5522→					}
  5523→				}(start, end)
  5524→			}
  5525→			wg.Wait()
  5526→		}

  5634→		// Sorting functionality
  5635→		let sortDirection = {};
  5636→		function sortTable(columnIndex) {
  5637→			const table = document.getElementById('uploadsTable');
  5638→			const tbody = document.getElementById('uploadsTableBody');
  5639→			const rows = Array.from(tbody.querySelectorAll('tr'));
  5640→			const header = table.querySelector('thead tr:first-child th:nth-child(' + (columnIndex + 1) + ')');
  5641→			const dataType = header.getAttribute('data-type');
  5642→
  5643→			// Toggle sort direction
  5644→			const currentDir = sortDirection[columnIndex] || 'none';
  5645→			sortDirection[columnIndex] = currentDir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
  5646→
  5647→			// Remove sort classes from all headers
  5648→			table.querySelectorAll('.sortable').forEach(h => {
  5649→				h.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');
  5650→			});
  5651→
  5652→			// Add sort class to current header
  5653→			header.classList.add(sortDirection[columnIndex]);
  5654→
  5655→			// Sort rows
  5656→			rows.sort((a, b) => {
  5657→				let aVal = a.cells[columnIndex].textContent.trim();
  5658→				let bVal = b.cells[columnIndex].textContent.trim();
  5659→
  5660→				// Special handling for Source column - sort by numeric import ID
  5661→				if (columnIndex === 7) {
  5662→					const aSourceID = a.cells[columnIndex].getAttribute('data-source-id');
  5663→					const bSourceID = b.cells[columnIndex].getAttribute('data-source-id');
  5664→					aVal = parseInt(aSourceID) || 0;
  5665→					bVal = parseInt(bSourceID) || 0;
  5666→					return sortDirection[columnIndex] === 'asc' ? aVal - bVal : bVal - aVal;
  5667→				}
  5668→
  5669→				// Numeric comparison
  5670→				if (dataType === 'number') {
  5671→					aVal = parseInt(aVal) || 0;
  5672→					bVal = parseInt(bVal) || 0;
  5673→					return sortDirection[columnIndex] === 'asc' ? aVal - bVal : bVal - aVal;
  5674→				}
  5675→
  5676→				// Date comparison
  5677→				if (dataType === 'date') {
  5678→					aVal = new Date(aVal).getTime();
  5679→					bVal = new Date(bVal).getTime();
  5680→					return sortDirection[columnIndex] === 'asc' ? aVal - bVal : bVal - aVal;
  5681→				}
  5682→
  5683→				// Text comparison
  5684→				if (sortDirection[columnIndex] === 'asc') {
  5685→					return aVal.localeCompare(bVal);
  5686→				} else {
  5687→					return bVal.localeCompare(aVal);
  5688→				}
  5689→			});
  5690→
  5691→			// Reappend sorted rows
  5692→			rows.forEach(row => tbody.appendChild(row));
  5693→		}
  5694→
  5695→		// Filtering functionality
  5696→		function filterTable() {

  5714→								cellText = cellText + ' ' + sourceID;

  5716→							if (!cellText.includes(filterValue)) {
  5717→								show = false;
  5718→							}
  5719→						}
  5720→					}
  5721→				});
  5722→				row.style.display = show ? '' : 'none';
  5723→			});

  5800→				const reader = response.body.getReader();

  5802→				let buffer = '';

  5812→					let lines = buffer.split('\n');
  5813→					buffer = lines.pop(); // Keep incomplete line in buffer
  5814→
  5815→					let currentEvent = null;

  5822→							currentData = line.substring(5).trim();
  5823→						} else if (line === '') {

  5825→							if (currentData) {

  5854→			} catch (err) {
  5855→				console.error('Import error:', err);
  5856→				status.className = 'import-status error';
  5857→				status.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;
  5858→				btn.disabled = false;
  5859→			}
  5860→		}
  5861→	</script>
  5862→</body>
  5863→</html>`
  5864→
  5865→	fmt.Fprint(w, html)
  5866→}
  5867→
  5868→// formatFileSize converts bytes to human-readable format
  5869→func formatFileSize(bytes int64) string {
  5870→	const unit = 1024
  5871→	if bytes < unit {
  5872→		return fmt.Sprintf("%d B", bytes)
  5873→	}
  5874→	div, exp := int64(unit), 0
  5875→	for n := bytes / unit; n >= unit; n /= unit {
  5876→		div *= unit
  5877→		exp++
  5878→	}
  5879→	return fmt.Sprintf("%.1f %cB", float64(bytes)/float64(div), "KMGTPE"[exp])
  5880→}
  5881→
  5882→// adminDeleteTrackHandler deletes a track and all associated data.
  5883→// POST /api/admin/delete
  5884→// Body: {"password": "xxx", "trackID": "abc123"} (password optional if session-authenticated)
  5885→func adminDeleteTrackHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
  5886→	if r.Method != "POST" {
  5887→		http.Error(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
  5888→		return
  5889→	}
  5890→
  5891→	if db == nil || db.DB == nil {
  5892→		http.Error(w, "Database not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
  5893→		return
  5894→	}
  5895→
  5896→	// Parse request
  5897→	var req struct {
  5898→		Password string `json:"password"`
  5899→		TrackID  string `json:"trackID"`
  5900→	}
  5901→
  5902→	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
  5903→		http.Error(w, "Invalid request body", http.StatusBadRequest)
  5904→		return
  5905→	}
  5906→
  5907→	// Check for admin access: session-based or password-based
  5908→	isSessionAdmin := false
  5909→	if user, ok := auth.GetUserFromContext(r.Context()); ok && user.IsAdmin {
  5910→		isSessionAdmin = true
  5911→	}
  5912→
  5913→	if !isSessionAdmin {
  5914→		// Fall back to password authentication
  5915→		if *adminPassword == "" {
  5916→			http.Error(w, "Admin endpoints are disabled - please login as admin", http.StatusForbidden)
  5917→			return
  5918→		}
  5919→		if req.Password != *adminPassword {
  5920→			http.Error(w, "Unauthorized", http.StatusUnauthorized)
  5921→			return
  5922→		}
  5923→	}
  5924→
  5925→	// Validate trackID
  5926→	if req.TrackID == "" {
  5927→		http.Error(w, "trackID is required", http.StatusBadRequest)
  5928→		return
  5929→	}
  5930→
  5931→	ctx := r.Context()
  5932→	err := db.DeleteTrack(ctx, req.TrackID)
  5933→	if err != nil {
  5934→		log.Printf("Error deleting track %s: %v", req.TrackID, err)
  5935→		http.Error(w, "Failed to delete track", http.StatusInternalServerError)
  5936→		return
  5937→	}
  5938→
  5939→	log.Printf("Admin deleted track: %s", req.TrackID)
  5940→
  5941→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
  5942→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
  5943→		"status":  "success",
  5944→		"message": "Track deleted successfully",
  5945→		"trackID": req.TrackID,
  5946→	})
```

> AGENT

This is a big inline HTML page (~780 lines). Converting it to a template file like the other admin pages would be a significant but clean refactor. The key challenge is that the uploads page has server-side data (totalCount, page, uploads rows) embedded in the HTML, unlike the other admin pages which fetch data via API calls from JS.

The cleanest approach is to convert it to a client-side rendered page (like admin-users.html, admin-realtime.html) that fetches uploads via the existing `/api/admin/uploads` API. But wait — let me check if that API returns JSON or if the handler IS the page.

    47→    .admin-tabs { display: flex; gap: 2px; margin-top: 10px; margin-bottom: 10px; background: var(--border-color); border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden; }
    48→    .admin-tabs a, .admin-tabs span { padding: 10px 20px; text-decoration: none; color: var(--text-secondary); background: var(--bg-card); font-weight: 500; font-size: 0.95em; transition: background 0.2s; }
    49→    .admin-tabs a:hover { background: var(--hover-bg); color: var(--text-primary); }
    50→    .admin-tabs a.active { background: var(--tab-active-bg); color: var(--tab-active-text); }
    51→
    52→    .controls { display: flex; gap: 10px; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
    53→    .controls input[type="text"], .controls select { padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.95em; }
    54→    .controls input[type="text"] { min-width: 250px; }
    55→    .controls button { padding: 8px 16px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer; font-size: 0.95em; }
    56→    .controls button:hover { background: var(--hover-bg); }
    57→    .controls .reload-btn { background: #4CAF50; color: white; border-color: #4CAF50; }
    58→    .controls .reload-btn:hover { background: #388E3C; }
    59→    .controls .add-btn { background: #2196F3; color: white; border-color: #2196F3; }
    60→    .controls .add-btn:hover { background: #1976D2; }
    61→
    62→    .summary { margin-bottom: 10px; color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9em; }
    63→
    64→    .table-wrap { overflow-x: auto; background: var(--bg-card); border-radius: 8px; box-shadow: var(--shadow); }
    65→    table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 0.85em; }
    66→    th { background: var(--th-bg); color: white; padding: 10px 12px; text-align: left; white-space: nowrap; position: sticky; top: 0; }
    67→    td { padding: 8px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color); }
    68→    tr:hover { background: var(--hover-bg); }
    69→    .sortable { cursor: pointer; user-select: none; position: relative; padding-right: 20px; }
    70→    .sortable:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1); }
    71→    .sortable::after { content: '\21C5'; position: absolute; right: 4px; opacity: 0.5; }
    72→    .sortable.asc::after { content: '\25B2'; opacity: 1; }
    73→    .sortable.desc::after { content: '\25BC'; opacity: 1; }
    74→
    75→    .col-id { width: 60px; }
    76→    .col-lang { width: 80px; }
    77→    .col-key { width: 220px; word-break: break-word; }
    78→    .col-value { max-width: 500px; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; cursor: pointer; }
    79→    .col-value:hover { white-space: normal; overflow: visible; position: relative; z-index: 10; background: var(--bg-card); box-shadow: var(--shadow); }
    80→    .col-date { width: 160px; }
    81→    .col-actions { width: 120px; white-space: nowrap; }
    82→
    83→    .pagination { display: flex; gap: 5px; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin-top: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
    84→    .pagination button { padding: 6px 12px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer; }
    85→    .pagination button:hover { background: var(--hover-bg); }
    86→    .pagination button.active { background: var(--tab-active-bg); color: white; border-color: var(--tab-active-bg); }
    87→    .pagination button:disabled { opacity: 0.4; cursor: not-allowed; }
    88→    .pagination select { padding: 6px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); }
    89→
    90→    .no-data { text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: var(--text-muted); }
    91→    .loading { text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: var(--text-muted); }
    92→
    93→    .btn-edit { background: #FF9800; color: white; border: none; padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 0.85em; }
    94→    .btn-edit:hover { background: #F57C00; }
    95→    .btn-delete { background: #f44336; color: white; border: none; padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 0.85em; margin-left: 4px; }
    96→    .btn-delete:hover { background: #d32f2f; }
    97→
    98→    /* Modal */
    99→    .modal-overlay { display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index: 1000; }
   100→    .modal-overlay.visible { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }
   101→    .modal { background: var(--bg-card); border-radius: 12px; padding: 24px; max-width: 900px; width: 95%; max-height: 90vh; overflow-y: auto; box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); }
   102→    .modal h3 { margin-bottom: 15px; }
   103→    .modal label { display: block; margin-bottom: 6px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-secondary); }
   104→    .modal input[type="text"] { width: 100%; padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.95em; margin-bottom: 12px; }
   105→    .modal textarea { width: 100%; min-height: 300px; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.9em; font-family: monospace; line-height: 1.5; margin-bottom: 12px; resize: vertical; }
   106→    .modal .btn-row { display: flex; gap: 10px; justify-content: flex-end; }
   107→    .modal .btn-save { background: #4CAF50; color: white; border: none; padding: 10px 24px; border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); cursor: pointer; font-size: 0.95em; }
   108→    .modal .btn-save:hover { background: #388E3C; }
   109→    .modal .btn-cancel { background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); border: 1px solid var(--border-color); padding: 10px 24px; border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); cursor: pointer; font-size: 0.95em; }
   110→    .modal .btn-cancel:hover { background: var(--hover-bg); }
   111→
   112→    .page-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 0; }
   113→    .page-header h1 { margin: 0; }
   114→    .back-to-map-btn { background: #2196F3 !important; color: white !important; padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); text-decoration: none !important; font-weight: 500; transition: background 0.2s; }
   115→    .back-to-map-btn:hover { background: #1976D2 !important; }
   116→
   117→    .lang-badge { display: inline-block; padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 4px; background: var(--tab-active-bg); color: white; font-size: 0.8em; font-weight: 600; }
   118→  </style>
   119→</head>
   120→<body>
   121→
   122→<div class="page-header">
   123→  <h1>Translations</h1>
   124→  <a href="/" class="back-to-map-btn">Back to Map</a>
   125→</div>
   126→
   127→<div class="admin-tabs" id="adminTabs"></div>
   128→
   129→<div class="controls">
   130→  <select id="langFilter" onchange="filterByLang()">
   131→    <option value="">All languages</option>
   132→  </select>
   133→  <input type="text" id="searchInput" placeholder="Search key or value..." onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')doSearch()">
   134→  <button onclick="doSearch()">Search</button>
   135→  <button onclick="clearSearch()">Clear</button>
   136→  <button class="add-btn" onclick="showAddModal()">+ Add Translation</button>
   137→  <button class="reload-btn" onclick="reloadTranslations()">Reload into Memory</button>
   138→  <span class="summary" id="summary"></span>
   139→</div>
   140→
   141→<div class="table-wrap">
   142→  <div class="loading" id="loading">Loading...</div>
   143→  <table id="dataTable" style="display:none">
   144→    <thead>
   145→      <tr>
   146→        <th class="col-id sortable" onclick="sortBy('id')">ID</th>
   147→        <th class="col-lang sortable" onclick="sortBy('language_code')">Lang</th>
   148→        <th class="col-key sortable" onclick="sortBy('key')">Key</th>
   149→        <th class="col-value">Value</th>
   150→        <th class="col-date sortable" onclick="sortBy('updated_at')">Updated</th>
   151→        <th class="col-actions">Actions</th>
   152→      </tr>
   153→    </thead>
   154→    <tbody id="tableBody"></tbody>
   155→  </table>
   156→  <div class="no-data" id="noData" style="display:none">No translations found</div>
   157→</div>
   158→
   159→<div class="pagination" id="pagination"></div>
   160→
   161→<!-- Edit/Add Modal -->
   162→<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalOverlay" onclick="closeModal()">
   163→  <div class="modal" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
   164→    <h3 id="modalTitle">Edit Translation</h3>
   165→    <input type="hidden" id="editId">
   166→    <label for="editLang">Language Code</label>
   167→    <input type="text" id="editLang" placeholder="e.g. en, cs, ja" maxlength="10">
   168→    <label for="editKey">Key</label>
   169→    <input type="text" id="editKey" placeholder="e.g. legend_safe">
   170→    <label for="editValue">Value</label>
   171→    <textarea id="editValue" placeholder="Translation text..."></textarea>
   172→    <div class="btn-row">
   173→      <button class="btn-cancel" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
   174→      <button class="btn-save" onclick="saveTranslation()">Save</button>
   175→    </div>
   176→  </div>
   177→</div>
   178→
   179→<script>
   180→const PASSWORD = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('password') || '';
   181→const AUTH_PARAM = PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';
   182→
   183→let currentSort = 'key';
   184→let currentOrder = 'asc';
   185→let currentSearch = '';
   186→let currentLang = '';
   187→let currentOffset = 0;
   188→let currentLimit = 50;
   189→let totalRows = 0;
   190→let availableLanguages = [];
   191→
   192→function buildAdminTabs() {
   193→  const tabs = [
   194→    { label: 'Users', href: '/admin/users' },
   195→    { label: 'Uploads', href: '/admin/uploads' },
   196→    { label: 'MCP Analytics', href: '/admin/mcp' },
   197→    { label: 'Realtime', href: '/admin/realtime' },
   198→    { label: 'Translations', href: '/admin/translations', active: true }
   199→  ];
   200→  const container = document.getElementById('adminTabs');
   201→  tabs.forEach(t => {
   202→    const href = t.href + (PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '');
   203→    container.innerHTML += `<a href="${href}" class="${t.active ? 'active' : ''}">${t.label}</a>`;
   204→  });
   205→}
   206→
   207→function doSearch() {
   208→  currentSearch = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.trim();
   209→  currentOffset = 0;
   210→  fetchData();
   211→}
   212→
   213→function clearSearch() {
   214→  document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
   215→  currentSearch = '';
   216→  currentOffset = 0;
   217→  fetchData();
   218→}
   219→
   220→function filterByLang() {
   221→  currentLang = document.getElementById('langFilter').value;
   222→  currentOffset = 0;
   223→  fetchData();
   224→}
   225→
   226→function sortBy(col) {
   227→  if (currentSort === col) {
   228→    currentOrder = currentOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
   229→  } else {
   230→    currentSort = col;
   231→    currentOrder = col === 'key' || col === 'language_code' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
   232→  }
   233→  currentOffset = 0;
   234→  fetchData();
   235→  updateSortIndicators();
   236→}
   237→
   238→function updateSortIndicators() {
   239→  document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
   240→    th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');
   241→  });
   242→  // Find the matching th
   243→  document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
   244→    const col = th.getAttribute('onclick')?.match(/sortBy\('(\w+)'\)/)?.[1];
   245→    if (col === currentSort) {
   246→      th.classList.add(currentOrder);
   247→    }
   248→  });
   249→}
   250→
   251→function escapeHtml(str) {
   252→  const div = document.createElement('div');
   253→  div.textContent = str;
   254→  return div.innerHTML;
   255→}
   256→
   257→function formatDate(val) {
   258→  if (!val) return '';
   259→  try {
   260→    const d = new Date(val);
   261→    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(val);
   262→    return d.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, '');
   263→  } catch { return String(val); }
   264→}
   265→
   266→async function fetchData() {
   267→  const loading = document.getElementById('loading');
   268→  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');
   269→  const noData = document.getElementById('noData');
   270→
   271→  loading.style.display = 'block';
   272→  table.style.display = 'none';
   273→  noData.style.display = 'none';
   274→
   275→  let url = `/api/admin/translations?limit=${currentLimit}&offset=${currentOffset}&sort=${currentSort}&order=${currentOrder}`;
   276→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   277→  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
   278→  if (currentLang) url += '&lang=' + encodeURIComponent(currentLang);
   279→
   280→  try {
   281→    const resp = await fetch(url);
   282→    if (!resp.ok) {
   283→      if (resp.status === 401) {
   284→        loading.textContent = 'Unauthorized - please login as admin or provide password';
   285→        return;
   286→      }
   287→      throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   288→    }
   289→
   290→    const json = await resp.json();
   291→    totalRows = json.total;
   292→    const data = json.data || [];
   293→
   294→    // Populate language dropdown if we have languages
   295→    if (json.languages && json.languages.length > 0 && availableLanguages.length === 0) {
   296→      availableLanguages = json.languages;
   297→      const select = document.getElementById('langFilter');
   298→      json.languages.forEach(lang => {
   299→        const opt = document.createElement('option');
   300→        opt.value = lang;
   301→        opt.textContent = lang;
   302→        if (lang === currentLang) opt.selected = true;
   303→        select.appendChild(opt);
   304→      });
   305→    }
   306→
   307→    loading.style.display = 'none';
   308→
   309→    if (data.length === 0) {
   310→      noData.style.display = 'block';
   311→      document.getElementById('summary').textContent = 'No results';
   312→      document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';
   313→      return;
   314→    }
   315→
   316→    // Build body
   317→    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
   318→    let html = '';
   319→    data.forEach(row => {
   320→      const valuePreview = row.value.length > 120 ? row.value.substring(0, 120) + '...' : row.value;
   321→      html += `<tr>
   322→        <td class="col-id">${row.id}</td>
   323→        <td class="col-lang"><span class="lang-badge">${escapeHtml(row.language_code)}</span></td>
   324→        <td class="col-key">${escapeHtml(row.key)}</td>
   325→        <td class="col-value" title="Click to edit" onclick="showEditModal(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}', ${JSON.stringify(row.value)})">${escapeHtml(valuePreview)}</td>
   326→        <td class="col-date">${formatDate(row.updated_at)}</td>
   327→        <td class="col-actions">
   328→          <button class="btn-edit" onclick="showEditModal(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}', ${JSON.stringify(row.value)})">Edit</button>
   329→          <button class="btn-delete" onclick="deleteTranslation(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}')">Del</button>
   330→        </td>
   331→      </tr>`;
   332→    });
   333→    tbody.innerHTML = html;
   334→    table.style.display = 'table';
   335→    updateSortIndicators();
   336→
   337→    const page = Math.floor(currentOffset / currentLimit) + 1;
   338→    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / currentLimit);
   339→    document.getElementById('summary').textContent = `${totalRows} translations | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;
   340→    buildPagination(totalPages, page);
   341→
   342→  } catch (err) {
   343→    loading.textContent = 'Error loading data: ' + err.message;
   344→  }
   345→}
   346→
   347→function escapeAttr(str) {
   348→  return str.replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
   349→}
   350→
   351→function buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {
   352→  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');
   353→  let html = '';
   354→  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(1)">First</button>`;
   355→  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage - 1})">Prev</button>`;
   356→  let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 3);
   357→  let end = Math.min(totalPages, start + 6);
   358→  start = Math.max(1, end - 6);
   359→  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
   360→    html += `<button class="${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="goToPage(${i})">${i}</button>`;
   361→  }
   362→  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage + 1})">Next</button>`;
   363→  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${totalPages})">Last</button>`;
   364→  html += ` <select onchange="changeLimit(this.value)">`;
   365→  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {
   366→    html += `<option value="${n}" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;
   367→  });
   368→  html += `</select>`;
   369→  container.innerHTML = html;
   370→}
   371→
   372→function goToPage(page) {
   373→  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;
   374→  fetchData();
   375→}
   376→
   377→function changeLimit(val) {
   378→  currentLimit = parseInt(val);
   379→  currentOffset = 0;
   380→  fetchData();
   381→}
   382→
   383→function showEditModal(id, lang, key, value) {
   384→  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Edit Translation';
   385→  document.getElementById('editId').value = id;
   386→  document.getElementById('editLang').value = lang;
   387→  document.getElementById('editLang').readOnly = true;
   388→  document.getElementById('editKey').value = key;
   389→  document.getElementById('editKey').readOnly = true;
   390→  document.getElementById('editValue').value = value;
   391→  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');
   392→  document.getElementById('editValue').focus();
   393→}
   394→
   395→function showAddModal() {
   396→  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Add Translation';
   397→  document.getElementById('editId').value = '';
   398→  document.getElementById('editLang').value = currentLang || '';
   399→  document.getElementById('editLang').readOnly = false;
   400→  document.getElementById('editKey').value = '';
   401→  document.getElementById('editKey').readOnly = false;
   402→  document.getElementById('editValue').value = '';
   403→  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');
   404→  document.getElementById('editLang').focus();
   405→}
   406→
   407→function closeModal() {
   408→  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('visible');
   409→}
   410→
   411→async function saveTranslation() {
   412→  const id = document.getElementById('editId').value;
   413→  const lang = document.getElementById('editLang').value.trim();
   414→  const key = document.getElementById('editKey').value.trim();
   415→  const value = document.getElementById('editValue').value;
   416→
   417→  if (!lang || !key) {
   418→    alert('Language code and key are required');
   419→    return;
   420→  }
   421→
   422→  try {
   423→    let url, method, body;
   424→    if (id) {
   425→      // Update existing
   426→      url = `/api/admin/translations/${id}`;
   427→      if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   428→      method = 'PUT';
   429→      body = JSON.stringify({ value });
   430→    } else {
   431→      // Create new
   432→      url = '/api/admin/translations';
   433→      if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   434→      method = 'POST';
   435→      body = JSON.stringify({ language_code: lang, key, value });
   436→    }
   437→
   438→    const resp = await fetch(url, {
   439→      method,
   440→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   441→      body
   442→    });
   443→
   444→    if (!resp.ok) {
   445→      const text = await resp.text();
   446→      throw new Error(text || 'HTTP ' + resp.status);
   447→    }
   448→
   449→    closeModal();
   450→    fetchData();
   451→  } catch (err) {
   452→    alert('Save failed: ' + err.message);
   453→  }
   454→}
   455→
   456→async function deleteTranslation(id, lang, key) {
   457→  if (!confirm(`Delete translation ${lang}.${key}?`)) return;
   458→
   459→  let url = `/api/admin/translations/${id}`;
   460→  if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   461→
   462→  try {
   463→    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
   464→    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   465→    fetchData();
   466→  } catch (err) {
   467→    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);
   468→  }
   469→}
   470→
   471→async function reloadTranslations() {
   472→  let url = '/api/admin/translations/reload';
   473→  if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   474→
   475→  try {
   476→    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST' });
   477→    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   478→    const json = await resp.json();
   479→    alert(json.message || 'Translations reloaded!');
   480→  } catch (err) {
   481→    alert('Reload failed: ' + err.message);
   482→  }
   483→}
   484→
   485→// Keyboard shortcut: Ctrl+S to save in modal
   486→document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
   487→  if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 's') {
   488→    if (document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.contains('visible')) {
   489→      e.preventDefault();
   490→      saveTranslation();
   491→    }
   492→  }
   493→  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
   494→    closeModal();
   495→  }
   496→});
   497→
   498→buildAdminTabs();
   499→fetchData();
   500→</script>
   501→</body>
   502→</html>
   503→
```

The frontend code looks correct. The issue must be on the backend — the API endpoints. Let me check the admin translations handler.

   137→// adminTranslationUpdateHandler updates a single translation.

   139→func adminTranslationUpdateHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {

   158→	var payload struct {

   162→		http.Error(w, "Invalid JSON", http.StatusBadRequest)
   163→		return

   167→	if err != nil {

> AGENT

The PUT works fine from curl. The issue must be in the frontend JavaScript. Let me look more carefully at the `showEditModal` call.

Looking at line 328 of admin-translations.html:
```js
onclick="showEditModal(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}', ${JSON.stringify(row.value)})"
```

The problem is `JSON.stringify(row.value)` — if the value contains a single quote or special characters, the onclick attribute could break. But more importantly, the `escapeAttr` function on line 348:

```js
function escapeAttr(str) {
  return str.replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
}
```

If a key contains characters like `&` or other special chars, the onclick could break. But the main issue is likely that keys with underscores or values with newlines/quotes break the inline `onclick` handler.

Let me test by opening the page in a browser context. Actually, let me check the browser console errors by looking at what happens when the Edit button is clicked. The `onclick` generates inline JS — if any value contains a backtick, single quote, or newline, the inline event handler breaks silently.

The fix is to use a data-driven approach instead of inline onclick handlers. Let me store the data in a JS array and reference by index.

> TOOL

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "    // Build body\n    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');\n    let html = '';\n    data.forEach(row => {\n      const valuePreview = row.value.length > 120 ? row.value.substring(0, 120) + '...' : row.value;\n      html += `<tr>\n        <td class=\"col-id\">${row.id}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-lang\"><span class=\"lang-badge\">${escapeHtml(row.language_code)}</span></td>\n        <td class=\"col-key\">${escapeHtml(row.key)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-value\" title=\"Click to edit\" onclick=\"showEditModal(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}', ${JSON.stringify(row.value)})\">${escapeHtml(valuePreview)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-date\">${formatDate(row.updated_at)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-actions\">\n          <button class=\"btn-edit\" onclick=\"showEditModal(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}', ${JSON.stringify(row.value)})\">Edit</button>\n          <button class=\"btn-delete\" onclick=\"deleteTranslation(${row.id}, '${escapeAttr(row.language_code)}', '${escapeAttr(row.key)}')\">Del</button>\n        </td>\n      </tr>`;\n    });",
  "new_string": "    // Build body\n    currentData = data;\n    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');\n    let html = '';\n    data.forEach((row, idx) => {\n      const valuePreview = row.value.length > 120 ? row.value.substring(0, 120) + '...' : row.value;\n      html += `<tr>\n        <td class=\"col-id\">${row.id}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-lang\"><span class=\"lang-badge\">${escapeHtml(row.language_code)}</span></td>\n        <td class=\"col-key\">${escapeHtml(row.key)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-value\" title=\"Click to edit\" onclick=\"editByIndex(${idx})\">${escapeHtml(valuePreview)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-date\">${formatDate(row.updated_at)}</td>\n        <td class=\"col-actions\">\n          <button class=\"btn-edit\" onclick=\"editByIndex(${idx})\">Edit</button>\n          <button class=\"btn-delete\" onclick=\"deleteByIndex(${idx})\">Del</button>\n        </td>\n      </tr>`;\n    });"
}

> AGENT

Done. PR #30 merged: https://github.com/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pull/30

The edit/delete buttons were broken because inline `onclick` handlers with `JSON.stringify(row.value)` broke when values contained quotes, newlines, or special characters. Fixed by storing the fetched data in a JS array and using simple index-based lookups instead.
